### X.W11.p3 (COHESION §0ge.1(iii) · OD-W11-1(a) LCP-STRUCTURAL; W11 ADDENDUM sixth) — ESCALATED: the trigger fired (lhci ×3 on CI after p1+p2 RED, third read `39492847080`); the prerendered shell was BUILT and measured, and the specified cure is impossible at the bytes inside this unit's writable set on BOTH of its gates. Hydration is not mismatch-free: the first view's inline styles are computed in setup from each visitor's environment, and Vue does not fix style mismatches in production. The shell paints at 48–60 ms, but lab LCP does not move (lantern ×3 5,876–5,901 vs p2's 5,768–6,034), because the final LCP candidate is emitted after hydration by the scene's own client commit. No product commit. The bytes are banked as a patch (ESC-W11p3-1)

SERVED MODEL: claude-opus-5-5 · 2026-10-20 · HEAD `8f4822853` (tranche-u).

1. **Crash-recovery.** ⟨`git status --porcelain -- vite.config.ts demo/app/index.html demo/app/main.ts scripts/build docs/tranches/X/execution/A/X-W11.md`⟩ → empty. Nothing inherited. `scripts/build/` did not exist.

Third read, by this seat: ⟨`gh run download 39492847080 -n lhci-report`⟩ @ `6bddf4224` (p2 is its ancestor), lhci job `118870976422` = failure → LCP `6886 · 6492 · 6487`, TBT `2299 · 2441 · 1622`, FCP `5401 · 5075 · 5072`. **RED, so NOT-NEEDED cannot be banked.** §0ge.1(iii) fires.
3. **What was built (measure-before-ship, all inside the writable set).**
   - `demo/app/main.ts`: one factory `createVjsApp(hydrate)` (createApp / createSSRApp, the same router, provides and dark store). The client boot runs under `!import.meta.env.SSR`. It hydrates `#app[data-prerendered]` only when `<html data-first-view="shell">`; otherwise it empties the root and client-renders.
   - `demo/app/index.html`: the fouc-guard decides the first view. It stamps `shell` only for `#/` with no URL seed, no stored `color-picker` seed and no reduced motion. A rule hides the shell before paint for every other first view.
   - `scripts/build/prerender-shell.ts`: a gh-pages `closeBundle` plugin. It runs an SSR build of the same config over `entry-server.ts`, with the stylesheets nulled because the server bundle renders markup only, then renders in a child process (`render-shell.mjs`, which holds the jsdom globals the hash router and stores read at import). It writes the markup into `#app` and the 8 empty body-teleport anchors before `</body>`, and refuses any non-body teleport.
   - `vite.config.ts`: the plugin is wired into gh-pages only. ⟨`guarded --light -- npx vite build --mode gh-pages --outDir <scratch>`⟩ → rc 0, `#app` carries 16,790 B of markup. ⟨`guarded --light -- npx vue-tsc -p tsconfig.demo.json --noEmit`⟩ → 0 errors.
4. **Gate "hydration without mismatch": RED.** The instrument was a transient one-browser probe with one context per cell, closed in `finally`, over a diagnostic build with `__VUE_PROD_HYDRATION_MISMATCH_DETAILS__: true`, run with ⟨`guarded --timeout 10 -- node probe.mjs <diag dist>`⟩.
   - The 4 shell cells ({412, 1440} × {light, dark}) all hydrate in place: the prerendered root node is kept (`shellKept true`), `pageerrors []`. But each logs `Hydration completed but contains mismatches.`, with **8 style mismatches (light) / 9 (dark)**.
   - The 4 client cells (PRM, stored seed, `#/palettes`, URL seed) show **0 mismatches** and render correctly.
   - Each mismatch, diffed at its first differing byte (⟨`probe2.json`⟩, light-1440), is an inline style that a setup-time computation derives from the visitor's environment:
     - (a) `touch-action: pan-y` on the server vs `none`/absent on the client. `useSpectrumPlateStyle.ts:41-43` and `ComponentSliders.vue:219-220` branch on `isTouchDevice`, so the value is per device.
     - (b) `--console-rest-ink` `oklch(24.21% …)` vs `oklch(26.33% …)`. `ConsoleRail.vue:137-146` resolves it from `inkAmbient` (the live ground) and `isDark`.
     - (c) dark only: `--space-title-ink` `oklch(30.78% …)` vs `oklch(82.13% …)` (ColorSpaceSelector), which depends on the scheme.
     - (d) the atmosphere canvas has `style=""` on the server vs the client's `auroraCssGradient` (`App.vue` `:style`, `useAtmosphereBoot`), which depends on the scheme and the ground.
   - Vue reports each with "this mismatch is check-only. The DOM will not be rectified in production". A dark visitor, or one on a different pointer class, would therefore keep the server's inks until each value next changes. Shipping that is a defect, not a residual.
   - The only cures are hydration-safe composables (environment-derived values applied after mount) in `demo/picker/**`, `demo/shared/ui/ColorSpaceSelector.vue`, `demo/app/App.vue` and `demo/app/composables/boot/**`. All of those are outside this unit's set. Narrowing the shell to "the visitors whose environment the render happens to equal" (light, first-visit ground, touch) would serve the lab device and almost no one else. That is a gate-shaped substitution and was refused. `data-allow-mismatch` is an allowlist (HIGH) and was refused.
5. **Gate "lhci LCP ≤ 2500 / TBT ≤ 300": RED, measured locally on the shell build.**
   - Instrument: p2's lantern instrument (⟨`guarded --timeout 15 -- sh ~/.dev-logs/p2/bin/lh-run.sh <shell dist> 9861 p3-shell 3`⟩: Lighthouse 12.6.1, mobile default throttling).
   - Readings ×3: LCP `5887 · 5876 · 5901`, TBT `329 · 329 · 385`, FCP `3305 · 3303 · 3307`, CLS `0` ×3. p2's AFTER on the same instrument was LCP `6034 · 5771 · 5768`, TBT `165 · 125 · 135`, FCP `4917 · 4837 · 4836`.
   - So the shell cuts FCP by ~1.5 s, leaves LCP flat, and raises TBT by ~200 ms (the hydration walk).
   - Cause, read from the lhr: the LCP element is still `div.pane-shell > div.glass-resting` (the picker plate). Phases are TTFB 453, load delay 1,164, render delay **4,269** ms. Observed (unthrottled) FCP is 97 ms, but observed LCP is 277 ms, which is **after** DOMContentLoaded at 242 ms. That is, the final candidate is emitted after the entry has run.
   - Discriminated by an LCP-entry probe (⟨`guarded --timeout 10 -- node lcp.mjs <shell dist>`⟩, 412×823 touch, one browser):
     - **entry JS aborted**: one candidate at **48 ms**, size 19,600 (the shell's plate).
     - **JS on**: a candidate at 60 ms, size 19,600, whose element is later **detached** (`el: null`, removed by the client commit), then the picker card at **224 ms**, size 20,032.
   - The lab LCP therefore follows the scene's post-hydration commit (the region swap and the plate's re-paint at +2 % area), not the HTML. That commit is in `demo/shell/**` / `demo/app/App.vue` (PaneSlot's one-rAF commit), outside this unit's set, and moving it is "deferring first-paint state", which §0ge.1 forbids.
6. **Not shipped.** Both gates are RED for causes outside the set, and shipping would land a production hydration that keeps wrong inks.
   - The bytes are banked whole as a patch: ⟨`shasum -a 256 ~/.dev-logs/p3/prerender-shell.patch`⟩ → `f1ef725ab4ce96aed758843cd20c8130bd229f302bbbf02e4b6750f4c3d06b2a`, 6 files, +283/−43. ⟨`git apply --check`⟩ applies clean at `8f4822853`.
   - The evidence is beside it: `~/.dev-logs/p3/{probe1.json,probe2.json,lcp1.txt,lh1.txt,r{1,2,3}.json}`.
   - The working tree was restored to HEAD for exactly this unit's four paths: ⟨`git status --porcelain -- vite.config.ts demo/app/index.html demo/app/main.ts scripts/build`⟩ → empty. None of them carried a sibling hunk at open.

### X.W11.h2b (COHESION §0gl.7 · DISPOSITION §8 E2 · O18-992) — DONE: the IDENTITY floor reads `min(0.35× pick C, Cmax(h, L))` from the library's gamut map; light + dark GREEN ×2 (`370d66c73`)

**Seat**: claude-opus-5-5 · 2026-10-21 · HEAD at open `96ce99541` (tranche-u). W11.md read whole (538 L); COHESION §0gl read (`:4570-4600`, §0gl.7 at `:4582`); this record's RESUME-seventh header through its unit plan and the `.h2` receipt (`:2444-2475`) read.

**Crash-recovery.** ⟨`git status --porcelain -- e2e/smoke/oracles/o18-contrast-census.spec.ts docs/tranches/X/execution/A/X-W11.md`⟩ → ∅. Nothing inherited.

**Anchors (true bytes).** ⟨`grep -n IDENTITY_C_FLOOR e2e/smoke/oracles/o18-contrast-census.spec.ts`⟩ → `425: const IDENTITY_C_FLOOR = 0.35;` and the assertions at `:1017-1018` (accent) and `:1043-1044` (trigger ink). Library export: ⟨`grep -n "export function mapColorToGamut" src/color/operations.ts`⟩ → `147:`. It holds L and H and bisects the greatest sRGB-representable C (32 steps). It is re-exported on `dist/subpaths/color.js`, the package surface the sibling oracle `o23-specimen-gamut-honesty.spec.ts:4` already imports.

**Acts, in order.**
1. **RED ×2 (BEFORE).** The first two attempts did not reach the assertion. With load at 29, the cold per-run vite on :8090 timed out `page.goto` at `:996` (light), and a sibling held :8091 (`EADDRINUSE`). So one warm server ran under the guard: ⟨`VITE_API_URL=http://localhost:8097 VJS_VITE_CACHE_DIR=…/.vite-e2e-8097 guarded --timeout 30 -- npx vite --port 8097`⟩. Then ⟨`VJS_E2E_PORT=8097 guarded --timeout 20 -- npx playwright test e2e/smoke/oracles/o18-contrast-census.spec.ts --project=smoke --workers=1 -g "IDENTITY leg"`⟩ ×2 → `1 failed · 1 passed` ×2 (36.4 s · 15.7 s). Light fails at `:1018`: `accent oklch(98.575448096959% 0.006955453228 31.584817895596deg); pick C 0.1062`, `Expected: >= 0.037180607112331766 · Received: 0.006968179459289377`. Dark passes ×2.
2. **The library Cmax measured** (node over `dist/subpaths/color.js`: `mapColorToGamut(oklch(L, 0.5, H), "srgb").channels[1]`). The token quantizes to `rgb(255 249 247)`. The spec's inline converter reads it as L `0.986178`, C `0.006968179`, H `39.46`. The library's Cmax at those coordinates is `0.006968168`. The library's own conversion of the same bytes gives C `0.006968156`. The 8-bit accent therefore sits ON the sRGB boundary. The two converters disagree by about 2e-8. Cmax at the token's unquantized coordinates is `0.007203`, which the 8-bit sample cannot resolve. So the floor reads the measured accent's own L and H.
3. **The cure** (`e2e/smoke/oracles/o18-contrast-census.spec.ts`, +32/−4):
   - an import of `mapColorToGamut` and `oklch` from `../../../dist/subpaths/color.js`;
   - `gamutMaxChroma(L, H)`, which is the library's map of an over-ceiling chroma request;
   - `identityChromaFloor(pickC, accent) = min(IDENTITY_C_FLOOR × pickC, gamutMaxChroma(accent.L, accent.H))`, with a comment citing COHESION §0gl.7. The accent assertion compares against that floor. The trigger-ink row scales the same floor by its measured effective α.
   - Both comparisons carry `GAMUT_CONVERTER_EPS = 1e-6`, the measured converter disagreement at the boundary (act 2). That is four orders below the 0.0372 floor.
   - `IDENTITY_C_FLOOR` (0.35) and both hue legs (≤ 12°, ≤ 15°) are unchanged. No assertion was deleted, skipped or loosened where 0.35× is reachable.
   ⟨`guarded --light -- npx eslint e2e/smoke/oracles/o18-contrast-census.spec.ts --max-warnings=0`⟩ → exit 0.
4. **GREEN ×2 (AFTER).** Same command as act 1, ×2 → `2 passed (26.9s)` · `2 passed (17.1s)`.
5. **The other legs.** ⟨`VJS_E2E_PORT=8097 guarded --timeout 25 -- npx playwright test e2e/smoke/oracles/o18-contrast-census.spec.ts --project=smoke --workers=1 --grep-invert "IDENTITY leg"`⟩ → `30 passed (1.1m)`, exit 0. Their bodies are byte-unchanged; the edit touches only the identity test and the new module-level helpers. The warm server was stopped (⟨`pkill -f "vite --port 8097"`⟩; :8097 not listening).
6. **Commit.** `370d66c73`, `test(X.W11.h2b O18-992): the IDENTITY floor reads min(0.35x pick C, Cmax(h, L)) from the library's gamut map (COHESION §0gl.7)`, pathspec = the spec alone.

**Act 4 — the cure, at the bytes (INTENT kept, recorded).** The wait counts only document-timeline animations: `.filter((a) => a.timeline instanceof DocumentTimeline)` before `.map((a) => a.finished)`. **Acts, in order.**
1. **Typecheck baseline.** ⟨`guarded --light --timeout 10 -- npm run typecheck | grep 'error TS'`⟩ → 12 errors, all in `o11-header-gates.spec.ts`, all in the tsconfig.e2e program (`noUncheckedIndexedAccess`):
   - `:185` TS2532 `d[3]`;
   - `:232-234` TS2532 `d[i]`;
   - `:251` ×3 TS2345 (`lum` destructures `number[]`);
   - `:303` ×4 TS18048/2532 (`c.ink[3]`, `gr[i]` in the `.slice().map()` composite);
   - `:314` TS2339 (`remove` on `Node`; `addStyleTag` returns `ElementHandle<Node>`).
2. **Root cure, using real types** (`4149cbfb7`):
   - `type RGB = readonly [number, number, number]` and `type RGBA = readonly [number, number, number, number]`.
   - `rgba()` returns `RGBA`. `groundRegions` builds `RGB[]`. `lum` and `contrast` take `RGB`.
   - The ink composite destructures `const [ir, ig, ib, a] = c.ink` and builds an `RGB` one channel at a time.
   - Typed-array reads go through `at = (i) => d[i] ?? NaN`. An out-of-range read poisons the ratio, and NaN fails `toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5)`. It is not a masking 0.
   - The veil node is removed through `n.parentNode?.removeChild(n)`.
   - There is no cast, no `any`, no ts-ignore, and no assertion was touched.
   - ⟨`npx eslint …o11-header-gates.spec.ts --max-warnings=0`⟩ → clean. ⟨`git diff --check`⟩ → clean.
3. **Typecheck after the cure.** ⟨`guarded --light --timeout 10 -- npm run typecheck`⟩ ×2 → `exit 0` and `0` `error TS` ×2. All four programs pass: lib, demo, test and e2e.
4. **Arm (a): the published rungs, read at the true bytes (glass 10.1.1, `node_modules/@mkbabb/glass-ui/dist/styles`).**
   - `tokens/glass.css`: `--glass-veil-ink: oklch(0.28 0.035 70)` (light), `--glass-veil-base: 0.14`, `--glass-veil-step: 0.04`. The rungs run wash 0.06 · quiet 0.10 · resting 0.14 · floating 0.18 · overlay 0.22, with `chassis` = wash and `dialog` = floating.
   - `glass/veil.css`: `@utility glass-plate`. Here `--glass-veil-rest` is `clamp(tier, tier + 2·step·max(0, (backdrop-luma − 0.6)/0.4), tier + 2·step)`, and the background is that α of the veil ink over `--card`.
   - `PaneShell.vue` mounts `<Card tier="resting">`, which gives `.glass-card{--glass-veil-tier: var(--glass-veil-resting)}`.
   - **INTENT at the bytes:** in light, the veil ink is a *dark* ink, so a "denser resting rung" darkens the plate under a dark caption. That is the opposite of what the ruling needs.
5. **Measured, not argued.** A transient probe (scratchpad `d3c/rungprobe.mjs`; the o11 caption-leg algorithm verbatim; one swiftshader Chromium; one context per rung, closed in `finally`; vite self-hosted on :9783 and stopped on exit) set `--glass-veil-tier: var(--glass-veil-<rung>)` on `.pane-shell__card` at light 390 over `/browse /extract /mix`. ⟨`guarded --timeout 20 -- node rungprobe.mjs 9783 … base,quiet,floating,overlay,dialog light 390`⟩ ×2 and ⟨`… wash …`⟩ ×2 gave byte-identical results between runs:

| rung (α) | Browse/Extract/Mix caption (worst region) | the second header on Browse / Extract |
|---|---|---|
| wash (0.06) | **3.31** rgb(178,64,133) | 6.65 / 7.30 |
| quiet (0.10) | **3.27** rgb(178,63,128) | 6.21 / 6.81 |
| resting (0.14, HEAD) | **3.22** rgb(178,62,124) | 5.80 / 6.35 |
| floating = dialog (0.18) | **3.10** rgb(174,60,119) | 5.45 / 5.95 |
| overlay (0.22) | **2.96** rgb(170,58,114) | 5.08 / 5.54 |

   - Contrast falls monotonically as the plate gets denser. The backdrop-luma knee never fires (`max(0,(0−0.6)/0.4)` = 0), and if it did it would darken further.
   - Arithmetic cross-check (WCAG, veil ink ≈ rgb(52,38,28)): even α 0 gives ≈ 3.4:1. Pure black on rgb(178,62,124) gives 3.87:1 (`.d3b` act 6).
   - **No published rung reaches 4.5:1, so the §0gm.4 relay arm fires.** No product byte was changed. `PaneHeader.vue`, `PaneShell.vue` and `shell.css` are untouched. Taking `floating` or `overlay` would have *lowered* the three cells, by 0.12 and 0.26, and also lowered the second-header cells.

Live delivery to glass is left to the orchestrator; glass-ui is READ-ONLY.

     - the four demo boot modules.
   - Nothing outside the closure is in the bundle, so **at the bytes the 185 KB is the closure**. ESC-W11p3b-1 called it "its 185 KB library graph", which assumed extra bytes outside the closure. There are none.
3. **What the closure carries (unminified region census, ⟨`NOMIN=1 … iife-measure2.mjs`⟩ → 254,511 B, split on rolldown's `//#region` markers).**
   - glass `aurora.js` 103,884 B. Of that, **one line is 85,080 B**: the GLSL fragment template `` `#version 300 es … ${Ce} … ` `` plus `` `${o}${a}${s}` ``.
   - value `color-grammar` 75,748 · `actions` 28,103 · `anchors` 11,556.
   - glass `color.glsl` 8,056 · `color.wgsl` 5,001.
   - The rest is ≤ 6.6 KB each.
   - In the glass source (`node_modules/@mkbabb/glass-ui/dist/aurora.js:121`), the shader sources are declarators of ONE `var Se = "…", Ce = "…", we = "…", <frag> = `…${Ce}…${p}${m}…`, L = {…}` statement. `L` (the config `deriveAurora` reads) shares that statement.
   - rolldown drops the unused bindings but keeps their template-literal initialisers. Their `${…}` interpolate bindings imported from the glsl/wgsl chunks, and coercing those is not provably free of side effects.
4. **The dead share, minified (⟨`node -e` over `~/.dev-logs/p4/iife-before.js`, sha256 `0f6f705e2608daa8…`⟩).**
   - Template literals carrying shader syntax (`vec[234]|uniform |fn |@group|struct `): **21 literals, 99,791 B raw, 34,349 B gz -9**.
   - The whole IIFE is 59,469 B gz, so the shader strings are **58 %** of the inline script's transfer and **47 % of the 72,821 B document** `.p3b` measured.
   - Bisection: ⟨an entry of only `export { deriveAurora } from "@mkbabb/glass-ui/aurora"`⟩ → `TOTAL 178707`. ⟨`export { cssToOklch } from "@mkbabb/glass-ui/color"`⟩ → `TOTAL 68387`, which is value's CSS colour grammar and which the seed parse truly needs. So ~100 KB comes from the dead aurora shaders, ~68 KB from the live parser, and ~17 KB from the derive plus the demo core.
   - The probe entry file was deleted after each run. ⟨`ls demo/app/composables/boot/`⟩ shows the 10 committed files only.
5. **Every in-set lever, measured (same instrument, `RDOPTS`/`MIN` env).**

   | lever (vite.config.ts `rolldownOptions` / `minify`) | TOTAL |
   |---|---|
   | as shipped | 185,632 |
   | `optimization.inlineConst {mode:"all", pass:3}` (and `pass:5`) | 180,626 |
   | `output.strictExecutionOrder: false` | 185,632 |
   | `treeshake {propertyReadSideEffects:false, unknownGlobalSideEffects:false}` | 185,620 |
   | object-form `minify` (oxc compress `maxIterations:10` + treeshake flags) | 302,265 / 307,458 (Vite's `build.minify` takes no object, so the output is not minified) |

   None of these removes the shader literals. The only other mechanisms are:
   - rewriting the producer's module in a build transform, or using an undeclared transitive minifier. Both are a local patch of producer bytes or a package-pin change, and both are forbidden.
   - moving the derive out of line, which changes §0gl.10's ruled "inlined at build" mechanism and is not N1's cure.

   Glass has no narrower entry to import from: ⟨`node -p` glass `package.json#exports`⟩ has no derive-only subpath, and ⟨`grep -l deriveAurora glass/dist/*.js`⟩ → `aurora.js` only.
6. **No edit was made.** The specified cure ("185 KB → only the `boot/seeded-ground.ts` import closure") is already true at the bytes and buys 0 B. The INTENT (cut the dead weight out of the document) is impossible inside this writable set without a forbidden act. Per METHOD I do not substitute. The order lock (N1 → N2 → N3, lhci ×3 on CI after each) means N2 and N3 are not opened, and N4 is conditional on N3.

- G2 bar (lint + `npm run typecheck` + oracle slate + `npm test`): **held** from `.h4b`/`.d3c` (typecheck 0 ×2, SLATE CLEAN ×2) and `.b` r5 (1478/1478 ×2). This seat wrote no product or test byte.

**ESC-W11p4-1 (to the orchestrator): N1 is impossible as specified, measured.** The 185,632 B inline script is exactly the import closure of `boot/seeded-ground.ts`. 99,791 B of it (34,349 B gz, 58 % of the script and about 47 % of the document) is glass's dead aurora GLSL/WGSL shader source. It cannot be shaken because it shares one `var` statement with the `deriveAurora` config and interpolates imported chunks. Rulable arms:
- **(a) producer row (glass, by mail).** Glass ships `deriveAurora` (and the config it reads) in a module free of shader strings, either a derive-only subpath or shader sources split into their own statements or chunk. The inline then drops to about 86 KB raw (about 25 KB gz), with value.js changing only its import specifier. ADOPT-AT-LANDING.
- **(b) in-repo, re-rules §0gl.10's mechanism.** The inline carries only the boot plus `parseUrlSeed`. The derive IIFE ships as a hashed classic asset that loads parser-blocking only when a `#/?color=` seed is present. That keeps the first-frame seeded ground (O28-114) and removes all 59 KB gz from every unseeded document, including lhci's `/`. It needs the writable set (`vite.config.ts`, `index.html`) it already has, plus a ruling that out-of-line on a seed is lawful under §0gl.10.
- **(c) lift the order lock.** N2 (lazy hydration, the 1,020 ms task) and N3 (modulepreload demotion) proceed now, with N1 held for (a) or (b). N2 and N3 are independent of N1's bytes.

The profile from `.p3b` act 10 stands as the critical-path map. This seat adds the measured split of the 185 KB: shader 99.8 K · parser 68.4 K · derive and core about 17 K.
